annual_revenue_small - annual_reagent_cost_small - annual_carrier_cost_small\n    )\n    annual_margin_bulk = (\n        annual_revenue_bulk - annual_reagent_cost_bulk - annual_carrier_cost_bulk\n    )\n    annual_margin_difference = annual_margin_bulk - annual_margin_small\n\n    return {\n        \"assay_id\": str(assay[\"assay_id\"]),\n        \"assay_name\": str(assay[\"assay_name\"]),\n
